</script><script type="text/javascript">//<!--
/* <![CDATA[ (head-cquotient.js) */
var CQuotient = window.CQuotient = {};
CQuotient.clientId = 'aadb-crocs_gb';
CQuotient.realm = 'AADB';
CQuotient.siteId = 'crocs_gb';
CQuotient.instanceType = 'prd';
CQuotient.activities = [];
CQuotient.cqcid='';
CQuotient.cquid='';
CQuotient.initFromCookies = function () {
	var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
	for(var i=0;i < ca.length;i++) {
	  var c = ca[i];
	  while (c.charAt(0)==' ') c = c.substring(1,c.length);
	  if (c.indexOf('cqcid=') == 0) {
		  CQuotient.cqcid=c.substring('cqcid='.length,c.length);
	  } else if (c.indexOf('cquid=') == 0) {
		  CQuotient.cquid=c.substring('cquid='.length,c.length);
		  break;
	  }
	}
}
CQuotient.getCQCookieId = function () {
	if(window.CQuotient.cqcid == '')
		window.CQuotient.initFromCookies();
	return window.CQuotient.cqcid;
};
CQuotient.getCQUserId = function () {
	if(window.CQuotient.cquid == '')
		window.CQuotient.initFromCookies();
	return window.CQuotient.cquid;
};
CQuotient.trackEventsFromAC = function (/* Object or Array */ events) {
try {
	if (Object.prototype.toString.call(events) === "[object Array]") {
		events.forEach(_trackASingleCQEvent);
	} else {
		CQuotient._trackASingleCQEvent(events);
	}
} catch(err) {}
};
CQuotient._trackASingleCQEvent = function ( /* Object */ event) {
	if (event && event.id) {
		if (event.type === dw.ac.EV_PRD_DETAIL) {
			CQuotient.trackViewProduct( {id:'', alt_id: event.id, type: 'raw_sku'} );
		} // not handling the other dw.ac.* events currently
	}
};
CQuotient.trackViewProduct = function(/* Object */ cqParamData){
	var cq_params = {};
	cq_params.cookieId = CQuotient.getCQCookieId();
	cq_params.userId = CQuotient.getCQUserId();
	cq_params.product = cqParamData.product;
	cq_params.realm = cqParamData.realm;
	cq_params.siteId = cqParamData.siteId;
	cq_params.instanceType = cqParamData.instanceType;
	
	if(CQuotient.sendActivity) {
		CQuotient.sendActivity(CQuotient.clientId, 'viewProduct', cq_params);
	} else {
		CQuotient.activities.push({activityType: 'viewProduct', parameters: cq_params});
	}
};
/* ]]> */
// -->
</script>

<!--
    ------------------------------------------------------------------
    REMOVE DOCUMENTATION COMMENTS BELOW WHEN DUPLICATING THIS TEMPLATE
    ------------------------------------------------------------------
    
    MEGA MENU LIST CONTENT ASSET TEMPLATE
    
    Expected hierarchy of html elements and classes for proper
    mega menu list styling:
    .mm-heading (should probably be a heading element: h2-h6)
    .mm-list (must be a sibling of the .mm-heading element, should be a list element: ul or ol)
        li (list item element, no classes needed. Its child should be a link, button, or plain text (e.g. <li>Item 1</li>)
    
    NOTE: To make a mega menu list an expandable/collapsible accordion 
    on mobile, apply a has-menu class to the mm-heading element.
    Javascript will add the necessary markup, styling and functionality
    to allow its list to expand and collapse on screens smaller than 960px wide.
    Example:
    <h5 class="mm-heading has-menu">By collection</h5>
    
    Namespace classes specific to this list can be added to the 
    heading and list elements. The general convention is
    mm-heading-[foo] and mm-list-[foo], where foo is unique to this
    mega menu list.
    
    Content assets are used for these lists because they are not
    expected to change as frequently as the promo section of the 
    mega-menu-[nn] content slots. This code can be copy/pasted
    into the appropriate areas of those content slot configs if 
    desired though.
-->

<!--
    ------------------------------------------------------------------
    REMOVE DOCUMENTATION COMMENTS BELOW WHEN DUPLICATING THIS TEMPLATE
    ------------------------------------------------------------------
    
    MEGA MENU LIST CONTENT ASSET TEMPLATE
    
    Expected hierarchy of html elements and classes for proper
    mega menu list styling:
    .mm-heading (should probably be a heading element: h2-h6)
    .mm-list (must be a sibling of the .mm-heading element, should be a list element: ul or ol)
        li (list item element, no classes needed. Its child should be a link, button, or plain text (e.g. <li>Item 1</li>)
    
    NOTE: To make a mega menu list an expandable/collapsible accordion 
    on mobile, apply a has-menu class to the mm-heading element.
    Javascript will add the necessary markup, styling and functionality
    to allow its list to expand and collapse on screens smaller than 960px wide.
    Example:
    <h5 class="mm-heading has-menu">Activity</h5>
    
    Namespace classes specific to this list can be added to the 
    heading and list elements. The general convention is
    mm-heading-[foo] and mm-list-[foo], where foo is unique to this
    mega menu list.
    
    Content assets are used for these lists because they are not
    expected to change as frequently as the promo section of the 
    mega-menu-[nn] content slots. This code can be copy/pasted
    into the appropriate areas of those content slot configs if 
    desired though.
-->
<h5 class="mm-heading mm-heading-collections text-uppercase text-bold">Activity</h5>

    .mm-heading (should probably be a heading element: h2-h6)
    .mm-list (must be a sibling of the .mm-heading element, should be a list element: ul or ol)
        li (list item element, no classes needed. Its child should be a link, button, or plain text (e.g. <li>Item 1</li>)

    Namespace classes specific to this list can be added to the 
    heading and list elements. The general convention is
    mm-heading-[foo] and mm-list-[foo], where foo is unique to this
    mega menu list.
    
    Content assets are used for these lists because they are not
    expected to change as frequently as the promo section of the 
    mega-menu-[nn] content slots. This code can be copy/pasted
    into the appropriate areas of those content slot configs if 
    desired though.
-->

and then makes a call to render the configured template with the returned recommended products: ================= -->
<script>
(function(){
// window.CQuotient is provided on the page by the Analytics code:
var cq = window.CQuotient;
if (cq && ('function' == typeof cq.getCQUserId) && ('function' == typeof cq.getCQCookieId)) {
var recommender = '[[&quot;Homepage-Test&quot;]]';
// cleaning up the leading/trailing brackets and quotes:
recommender=recommender.slice(8, recommender.length-8);
var separator = '|||';
var slotConfigurationUUID = 'a5dc27d6ae1fa4896ed2304e5a';
var contextAUID = '';
var contextSecondaryAUID = '';
var contextAltAUID = '';
var contextType = '';
var anchorsArray = [];
var contextAUIDs = contextAUID.split(separator);
var contextSecondaryAUIDs = contextSecondaryAUID.split(separator);
var contextAltAUIDs = contextAltAUID.split(separator);
var contextTypes = contextType.split(separator);
var slotName = 'home-slot-20';
var slotConfigId = '20190509_Einstein_test';
var slotConfigTemplate = 'slots/recommendation/carouselSlot.isml';
if (contextAUIDs.length == contextSecondaryAUIDs.length)
{
for (i = 0; i < contextAUIDs.length; i++) {
anchorsArray.push({id: contextAUIDs[i], sku: contextSecondaryAUIDs[i], type: contextTypes[i], alt_id: contextAltAUIDs[i]});
}
} else {
anchorsArray = [{id: contextAUID, sku: contextSecondaryAUID, type: contextType, alt_id: contextAltAUID}];
}
var urlToCall = '/on/demandware.store/Sites-crocs_gb-Site/en_GB/CQRecomm-Start';
var params = {
userId: cq.getCQUserId(),
cookieId: cq.getCQCookieId(),
anchors: anchorsArray,
ccver: '1.01'
};
if (cq.getRecs) {
cq.getRecs(cq.clientId, recommender, params, cb);
} else {
cq.widgets = cq.widgets || [];
cq.widgets.push({
recommenderName: recommender,
parameters: params,
callback: cb
});
}
};
function cb(parsed) {
var arr = parsed[recommender].recs;
if (arr && 0 < arr.length) {
var filteredProductIds = '';
for (i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
filteredProductIds = filteredProductIds + 'pid' + i + '=' + encodeURIComponent(arr[i].id) + '&';
}
filteredProductIds = filteredProductIds.substring(0, filteredProductIds.length - 1);//to remove the trailing '&'
var formData = 'auid=' + encodeURIComponent(contextAUID)
+ '&scid=' + slotConfigurationUUID
+ '&' + filteredProductIds;
var request = new XMLHttpRequest();
request.open('POST', urlToCall, true);
request.setRequestHeader('Content-type', 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded');
request.onreadystatechange = function() {
if (this.readyState === 4) {
// Got the product data from DW, showing the products now by changing the inner HTML of the DIV:
var divId = 'cq_recomm_slot-' + slotConfigurationUUID;
document.getElementById(divId).innerHTML = this.responseText;
//find and evaluate scripts in response:
var scripts = document.getElementById(divId).getElementsByTagName('script');
if (null != scripts) {
for (var i=0;i<scripts.length;i++) {//not combining script snippets on purpose
var srcScript = document.createElement('script');
srcScript.text = scripts[i].innerHTML;
srcScript.asynch = scripts[i].asynch;
srcScript.defer = scripts[i].defer;
srcScript.type = scripts[i].type;
srcScript.charset = scripts[i].charset;
document.head.appendChild( srcScript );
document.head.removeChild( srcScript );
}
}
}
};
request.send(formData);
request = null;
}
};
})();
</script>

	// Make digicert code accessible
	var digicert = {
		observer: null,
		el: $('#DigiCertClickID_NM9tWpZ8'),
		callback: function() {
			digicert.el.find('img').attr({
				'aria-label': 'DigiCert EV Secure Seal; Press enter to verify.',
				'role': 'button'
			});
			// remove observer if it triggered the callback
			if (digicert.observer) digicert.observer.disconnect();
		}
	};

	if (digicert.el.find('img').length) {
		// digicert img already loaded
		digicert.callback();
	} else {
		// no img yet, add observer
		digicert.observer = new MutationObserver(digicert.callback);
		digicert.observer.observe(digicert.el[0], {childList: true});
	}

	$(document).ready(function() {
		var mainPage = $(".global-viewport"),
			cbox = $("#colorbox"),
			cboxClose = $("#cboxClose"),
			cboxCloseEvents = "keypress.cbox_closebtn"; // this allows focus to be set back to triggering element *ONLY* when user is interacting via keyboard
		
		$.extend($.colorbox.settings, {
			transition: "none",
			maxWidth: "95%",
			maxHeight: "95%",
			scrolling: false
		});
		
		// Add accessibility attributes to colorbox markup
		cbox.attr({
			"role": "dialog",
			"tabindex": "-1",
			"aria-modal": "true",
			"aria-hidden": "true"
		});
		// make close "button" capable of receiving focus (for elements that aren't natively so, like div and span)
		cboxClose.attr({
			"role": "button",
			"tabindex": "0"
		});
		// Event triggered after colorbox modal is revealed.
		$(document).on("cbox_complete", function() {
			// temporarily remove page content from tab order for screen readers
			mainPage.attr("aria-hidden", "true");
			// make modal available to screen readers and set focus to introduce tab order
			cbox.attr("aria-hidden", "false").focus();
			// handle keyboard event on close button (note: plugin already handles escape key)
			cboxClose.on(cboxCloseEvents, function(ev) {
				var key = $.ui.keyCode;
				if (ev.keyCode === key.ENTER || ev.keyCode === key.SPACE) {
					ev.preventDefault(); // prevent page scroll default for space keypress
					$.colorbox.close();
					// return focus to link that spawned the modal window (fails silently if colorbox was opened programatically)
					$.colorbox.element()[0].focus();
				}
			});
		});
		// Event triggered after colorbox modal is closed.
		$(document).on("cbox_closed", function() {
			// make page tabbable again
			mainPage.attr("aria-hidden", "false");
			// hide colorbox modal from screen readers
			cbox.attr("aria-hidden", "true");
			// remove close button listener
			cboxClose.off(cboxCloseEvents);
		});
	});
</script>

		function getItems(data){

		function getCookieValue(sKey,bool) {
			var cookieValue = decodeURIComponent(document.cookie.replace(new RegExp("(?:(?:^|.*;)\\s*" + encodeURIComponent(sKey).replace(/[\-\.\+\*]/g, "\\$&") + "\\s*\\=\\s*([^;]*).*$)|^.*$"), "$1")) || null;
			if(arguments.length > 1 && bool === true){
				cookieValue = JSON.parse(cookieValue);
			}
			return cookieValue;

		function getProfile(){
			try{
				var profile = {
					"suid" : getCookieValue('suid'),
					"sid" : getCookieValue('sid'),
					"customerNo" : (function(window){try{return getValueAsString(window.app.config.customerNo);}catch(e){return '';}})(window),

				customer = getCookieValue('customer', true);

					for(property in customer){
						profile[property] = customer[property];
					}
				}
				// remove null, ''
				for(property in profile){
					if(!profile[property]){
						delete profile[property];
					}
				}
			
				return profile;				
			}catch(e){
				log('getProfile',e,'error');
				return '';
			}

		function getItemDiscount(data){
			var len = data.length;
			var discount = 0;
			for(var i=0;i<len;i++){
				discount -= data[i].discount;
			}
			return discount;
		}
		
		function getValueAsString(data){
			if(typeof data !== 'undefined'){
				if(typeof data === 'string'){
					return data;
				}else{
					return JSON.stringify(data);
				}
			}else{
				return '';
			}
		}
		
		function getJSONdata(obj){
			var globalMboxObj = {};
			for(k in obj){
				var value = obj[k];
				if(value){
					globalMboxObj[k] = value;
				}
			}
			return globalMboxObj;
		}

					"crx_pd_paymentMethod" : (function(window){try{return getValueAsString(window.app.order.data.paymentMethod);}catch(e){return '';}})(window),
					"crx_ac_orderNumber" : (function(window){try{return getValueAsString(window.app.order.data.orderNumber);}catch(e){return '';}})(window),
					"crx_ac_orderUUID" : (function(window){try{return getValueAsString(window.app.order.data.orderUUID);}catch(e){return '';}})(window),
					"crx_ac_orderToken" : (function(window){try{return getValueAsString(window.app.order.data.orderToken);}catch(e){return '';}})(window),
					"crx_ac_rewardsTotal" : (function(window){try{return getValueAsString(window.app.globals.rewards.rewardsTotal);}catch(e){return '';}})(window),
					"crx_ac_rewardsTotalFormatted" : (function(window){try{return getValueAsString(window.app.globals.rewards.rewardsTotalFormatted);}catch(e){return '';}})(window),
					"crx_testSegment" : (function(window){try{return getValueAsString(window.app.customer.testSegment);}catch(e){return '';}})(window)

				};

			// rectify data layer versions
			for(k in globalMboxParamsNewDL){
				if(!globalMboxParamsNewDL[k] && globalMboxParamsOldDL[k]){
					globalMboxParams[k] = globalMboxParamsOldDL[k];
					log('not in new DL: '+k,null,'warn');
				}else if(globalMboxParamsNewDL[k] !== 'undefined' && globalMboxParamsNewDL[k] !== ""){
					globalMboxParams[k] = globalMboxParamsNewDL[k];
				}
			}
			
			var JSONdata = getJSONdata(globalMboxParams);
			
			log('globalMboxParamsOldDL',globalMboxParamsOldDL,'log');
			log('globalMboxParamsNewDL',globalMboxParamsNewDL,'log');			
			log('JSONdata',JSONdata,'log');
			
			// Pass data to Target Global Mbox
			window.targetPageParams = function() {
				return JSONdata;
			};
		
			initSettings();
		}
	
		// Set Glboal Target Settings
		function initSettings(){
			window.targetGlobalSettings = { 
			  bodyHidingEnabled : false
			};
		
			initAtjs()
		}
